Completion and intervention activity characterisation

Revision 1. Public BSEE snapshots; descriptive record counts.

Executive Summary

The supplied snapshots establish record counts and descriptive distributions.

  • completion records: 72844
  • eor completion records: 9245
  • eor records: 45538
  • eor recorded wells: 21148
  • workover records: 4103
  • workover records with api: 4103
  • workover records per recorded well: 0.2
  • war records: 369775
  • candidate war records: 230402
  • high count wells: 864
  • low count wells: 0

These counts do not establish intervention event rates or completion design performance.

Revision history

RevisionDateWhat changed and why
12026-10-14Initial record characterisation with source digests and explicit coverage limits.

Revision record retained with the analysis criteria.

Method and parameters

MeasureBasis
frequency_basisrecords per recorded API12; no exposure-time denominator
completion_era_basisAPI Lookup COMPLETION_DATE
eor_year_basisBOREHOLE_STAT_DT; not intervention event date
war_year_basisWAR_START_DT; not an intervention start
depth_basisunique nonnegative borehole WATER_DEPTH, in feet
field_basisunique API Lookup BOTM_FLD_NAME_CD; ambiguous and unmatched are unknown
bin_basisdescriptive water-depth boundaries and calendar cohorts; no design criterion
candidate_basislegacy intervention selection in lower_tertiary.ops_timeline; raw codes only
outlier_basisstrict below lower or above upper linear empirical quantile; ties excluded; flags use unrounded values; observed records only

All frequencies are record counts over the supplied archive.

Parameters: {"candidate_codes": ["PA", "REC", "ST", "TA", "WO"], "depth_edges": [1000, 3000, 5000, 10000], "era_edges": [2000, 2010, 2020], "outlier_quantiles": [0.05, 0.95], "workover_codes": ["W"]}

Field record frequencies; unequal observation periods preclude rate comparisons.

Data limits and coverage

  • Completion status and reported subsea flag are characterised separately; general completion design types are not supplied by these tables.
  • API completion rows may describe production intervals or repeated historical listings; counts do not establish unique completion installations.
  • EOR years use borehole-status dates and cannot establish workover event timing. Completion years use recorded completion dates, not spud dates.
  • WAR rows are weekly reporting records, not separate interventions. Missing properties and unknown activity-code meanings remain explicit.
  • Workover selection uses EOR operation code W, whose source code table labels it WORKOVER. WAR candidate selection is an interpretation, not a published WAR classification.
  • Plug-back classification and intervention triggers are not established by the selected structured data; narrative text is not interpreted.
  • Recorded zero workovers indicate no W reports in this archive for an observed API12, not absence of interventions over the well life. Observation exposure and well-age effects are not established.
  • Field and depth attribution use a current snapshot; ambiguous metadata stays unknown. Historical reassignment is not reconstructed.
  • Rates, durations, production correlations and exposure-normalised outliers wait for issue #1063. Completion-type, well-age and field correlations are not computed here.
  • Coverage is the supplied snapshot, not a census of all operations. Calendar changes can reflect reporting coverage and cannot establish changes in engineering practice.
